- **aio-message-bridge**: Give Claude Code an event loop. A local HTTP + WebSocket relay plus the Monitor tool let anything OUTSIDE Claude — a CLI in another terminal, a mobile app, a webhook, another running program, a web page — push events to Claude mid-task and receive pushes back, in real time. Inbound: the external client POSTs an event, the relay prints a MSG:: line to stdout, the Monitor tool surfaces it as a notification. Outbound: Claude POSTs a push, the relay broadcasts it over WebSocket to every connected client. The protocol is generic and language-agnostic; a ready-to-run reference relay is included. - **aio-patch-compile**: Apply tools/pipeline/patches.json + bun build --compile → patched claude binary at dist/&lt;arch&gt;/claude. Default: current host arch only (fast inner-loop ~1.2s). Opt-in --target=all (4 platforms) or csv list (e.g. --target=darwin-arm64,linux-amd64). If cli.js for a target arch is not yet extracted, transitively calls extract first. Strict by default (fails fast on MISSING patches). ([aiocean/claude-plugins](https://github.com/aiocean/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/aio-claude-toolkit/skills/aio-patch-compile) · ★4)
- **aio-patch-control**: Scaffold a reference HTTP control-channel gateway (sample shell clients + bun TUI) into &lt;project&gt;/control/, OR print the control-channel protocol docs. NOT a generic abstraction — adapt to your own transport (WebSocket / ZMQ / named pipes) if needed. ([aiocean/claude-plugins](https://github.com/aiocean/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/aio-claude-toolkit/skills/aio-patch-control) · ★4)

([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/codex/.agents/skills/aipt-investigate-failure) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **aipt-learn-from-outcomes**: W12 Use verified outcomes to improve the product or team. Inputs: Audited runs; Outcomes; Failed approaches; Current versions. - **aipt-method-accessible-interaction-design**: Accessible interaction design, draft versus preview versus confirmation versus executed action, keyboard operation, non-color status, perceivable feedback, correction path, undo, handoff back to people, observed check versus untested claim. Use when specifying or reviewing how people see, operate and correct an AI feature, including the task before and after the AI exchange. Produces an interaction specification with a control inventory, four-state actions, feedback and correction paths, handoffs, and reproducible checks each labeled observed or untested. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-accessible-interaction-design) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-adversarial-challenge**: Challenge, objection, counterargument, falsification test, stress-test an assumption, pre-mortem, red-team a proposal, devil's advocate. Write material challenges to a frozen decision packet. Each names the contested premise, the counterevidence or gap, the consequence under the frozen criteria and the resolving test. Keep challenges independent and materially different within a bounded round budget (default two). Use when a consequential proposal rests on uncertain evidence or disputed alternatives. Not for ruling on challenges (aipt-method-evidentiary-adjudication) or applying delegation. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-adversarial-challenge) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-ai-responsibility-allocation**: Responsibility allocation, step ownership, fixed code versus model inference versus adaptive investigation versus human decision, loop budget, termination rule, permission check placement. Use when designing or reviewing a feature or workflow step by step to decide which mechanism is responsible for each step, to justify any adaptive behavior with a named unknown, to keep deterministic permission checks in code, and to bound every loop with a budget and a termination rule. Produces an allocation table as a proposal artifact. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-ai-responsibility-allocation) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-context-files-architecture**: Context files, agent operating files, root guidance, host-specific guidance, specialist role files, command files, skill files, layering, maintainability. Use when a repository or personal workflow needs better operating context for coding assistants, when existing files are vague, stale or bloated, or when universal guidance must be separated from project-specific guidance. Distinguishes file roles, decides what belongs where, keeps files concise and runnable, and proposes a minimal expandable file set. Not for one-off prompt wording or files nobody will maintain. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-context-files-architecture) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-controlled-learning**: Controlled learning, skill change proposal, workflow change proposal, repeated failure, verified outcomes, held-out tests, rollback version, protected policy, separate version, anecdote is not a rule. Use when audited results show the same failure recurring across independent cases and a change to a skill, role body or workflow recipe is proposed to address it. Produces a learning proposal on its own version with the failure statement, affected invariants, expected improvement, held-out tests and rollback; blocks any change to protected authority, exclusions, privacy boundaries or thresholds. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-controlled-learning) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-corpus-grounded-onboarding**: Onboarding, handoff absorption, corpus reading, codebase or document-set orientation, mental model, invariants, readiness check. Use when taking over a project, repository, document pack or handoff from a person or another agent and you must understand purpose, workflows, architecture, invariants and gaps before acting. Builds a structured product and system model, separates stated facts from strong inferences and unverified assumptions, marks operational boundaries, and states readiness honestly. Not for quick summaries or when no real corpus exists. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-corpus-grounded-onboarding) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-cost-and-latency-analysis**: Cost per completed task, latency distribution, p95, critical path, retries, dated rates, unknown rates, tokens versus outcomes. Use when a role must state what a feature costs per task that actually completed, or how long users wait end to end, from observed usage, timed traces and dated rate sources. Counts retries, retrieval, tool charges and review time; keeps unknown rates unknown instead of zero; reports latency as a measured end-to-end distribution; never sums parallel stage percentiles; separates critical-path, queued and user-review delay. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-cost-and-latency-analysis) · ★2 · 3 copies)
